{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"path\": \"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_5/projects/structfuse-context/report.md\", \"content\": \"# Handover — 2026-05-14\\n\\n> 由 Agent 生成，每次状态变更后自动更新。硬限 150 行。\\n\\n## 运行中\\n| exp_id | 假设 | 状态 | 关键指标 | W&B |\\n|--------|------|------|---------|-----|\\n| m2_full_qwen_continued | M2 Qwen continued: 继续 m2_full_qwen 的剩余 runs (random_s44 + embedding×3 + dataflow×3)。原实验 cron 在 s42 退出时自动设为 ANALYZING，但链仍在运行。 | — | — | — |\\n| m2_full_starcoder_resumed | M2 StarCoder resumed: 继续 m2_full_starcoder 的 8 sequential runs (random×2 + embedding×3 + dataflow×3)。原实验因 cron timeout 标记为 failed，实际 PID 519791 正常运行。 | — | — | — |\\n| m1_reextract_v2 | M1 重提取 v2：恢复 28,276 个因运行时瞬态文件系统故障失败的 instances（82 repos）。CodeQL pack 已修复，端到端测试通过。预期将训练数据从 15,897 提升至 ~44,173。D022 批准。 | — | — | — |\\n\\n## 待处理\\n- plan_018_bm25: BM25 negative baseline: Dream 审稿三档一致认定缺 BM25 baseline 是论文最大结构性缺陷。Phase 1 (CPU): 对 M2 训练语料建 BM25 索引，挖掘 BM25 hard negatives，生成训练数据。Phase 2 (GPU): 用 BM25 negatives 训练 Qwen + StarCoder 各 1 seed，对比 R@1。（排队等待 GPU）\\n- plan_015_dynamic: Dynamic negative mining (epoch-level re-mining): 每 epoch 重新用当前 encoder embedding 挖掘 negatives，验证 static mining 是否足够。缩减版：只跑 dynamic_ep × 1 seed (Qwen, s42)，~11h 单卡。若 dynamic ≈ static（预期 <1pp 差异），在 Limitation 段排除 confound。（排队等待 GPU）\\n- plan_005_stratified（准备中）\\n\\n## 近期结论\\n- **plan_017_literature** (success): Inference-time graph method comparison: 8 papers surveyed (CodexGraph, LocAgent, RANGER, GraphCoder, ARISE, RepoGraph, CodeGRAG, CodeRAG). Discussion v2 drafted, all citations verified. Conclusion: training-time vs inference-time complementary.\\n- **m2_full_starcoder** (failed): Full M2 StarCoder: 3 strategies × 3 seeds on full M1 data. Cross-encoder validation — if embedding > random holds for both Qwen and StarCoder, method is encoder-agnostic. Resolves plan_013 pilot negative (dataflow encoder-dependent). ← 基于 plan_013_starcoder\\n  [m2_starcoder_status_check](/api/workspace-files/projects/structfuse-context/artifacts/m2_full_starcoder/report/m2_starcoder_status_check.md)\\n- **neg_difficulty_analysis** (success): Negative difficulty distribution analysis: 用预训练 Qwen2.5-Coder-3B 和 StarCoder2-3B 计算 3 策略 (random/embedding/dataflow) 的 query-negative/positive cosine similarity，分析 pretrained representation separability 跨编码器差异。MAX_LENGTH=2048 匹配训练设置。\\n  [neg_difficulty_margin_comparison](/api/workspace-files/projects/structfuse-context/artifacts/neg_difficulty_analysis/chart/neg_difficulty_margin_comparison_meta.txt) | [neg_difficulty_margin_comparison](/api/workspace-files/projects/structfuse-context/artifacts/neg_difficulty_analysis/chart/neg_difficulty_margin_comparison_meta_01876a.txt) | [neg_difficulty_similarity_dist](/api/workspace-files/projects/structfuse-context/artifacts/neg_difficulty_analysis/chart/neg_difficulty_similarity_dist_meta.txt) | [neg_difficulty_ratio_heatmap](/api/workspace-files/projects/structfuse-context/artifacts/neg_difficulty_analysis/chart/neg_difficulty_ratio_heatmap_meta.txt)\\n\\n## 趋势\\n成功 11 / 负向 2 / 失败 3 / 共 30\\n\\n## 下一步\\n（无活跃 idea，请通过 ideas.md 添加）\\n\\n## 资源状态\\nbjb1-42106: 无 GPU 信息\\n\\n## 告警\\n- p2b-smoke-test: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- p2b-smoke2: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- p2b-smoke3: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- p2b-smoke4: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- p2b-smoke5: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- p2b-smoke6: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- p2b-smoke7: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- p2b-smoke8: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- p2b-all-9runs: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- mvp_p2b_contrastive_training: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- mvp_p2b_contrastive_v2: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- p2b_eval_all: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- m2_early_pilot: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- m2_early_pilot_v2: 训练失败 — unknown\\n- m2_early_pilot_v3: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- m2_full_starcoder: 训练失败 — unknown\\n- m1_reextract: 训练失败 — unknown\\n\", \"total_chars\": 3504, \"truncated\": false}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"}

DFG extraction but function-level | Code generation (cross-lingual) | Multiple PLs | Only method with training-time component (GNN soft prompts); but graph also used at inference |\\n| **StructFuse** (Ours) | Structure-Grounded Hard Negatives via CodeQL Data-Flow | — | Inter-procedural data-flow graph (CodeQL taint tracking) | **Training-time only** | **Yes. Full inter-procedural** (source→sink across call boundaries) | Code retrieval (contrastive training) | Large-scale repos with CodeQL | Zero inference overhead; graph used offline for negative mining; inter-proc data-flow |\\n\\n## Discussion 要点\\n\\n### StructFuse vs Inference-Time Graph Methods: 互补性与差异性\\n\\n1. **Cost-accuracy tradeoff**: Inference-time methods (CodexGraph, RANGER, RepoGraph) achieve high precision by constructing and querying graphs per request, but incur significant latency (graph construction + traversal + LLM calls). StructFuse amortizes all graph computation to training time—once the encoder is trained with structure-grounded negatives, inference is a single embedding lookup with zero graph overhead. This makes StructFuse suitable for latency-sensitive production retrieval where inference-time graph methods are impractical.\\n\\n2. **Data-flow depth**: Most inference-time methods use structural graphs (call graphs, class hierarchies, file dependencies). Only GraphCoder and ARISE incorporate data/control flow, but limited to **intra-procedural** scope. StructFuse uniquely leverages **inter-procedural** data-flow via CodeQL's taint tracking, capturing cross-function semantic relationships (e.g., a sanitizer function that transforms data between source and sink across call boundaries). This deeper semantic signal generates harder, more informative negatives.\\n\\n3. **Complementarity**: StructFuse and inference-time methods are orthogonal and composable. A StructFuse-trained encoder can serve as the embedding backbone for systems like RANGER or CodexGraph, providing better initial retrieval that reduces the graph agent's search space. Conversely, inference-time graph methods can provide reranking on top of StructFuse retrieval for tasks requiring maximum precision.\\n\\n4. **Scalability model**: Inference-time methods scale linearly with query volume (each query triggers graph operations). StructFuse's cost is front-loaded: CodeQL analysis runs once per repository update, and the resulting negatives improve the encoder permanently. For high-QPS code search services, this one-time investment yields compounding returns.\\n\\n5. **Task generality vs specialization**: Inference-time graph agents (CodexGraph, RepoGraph) are task-agnostic but require powerful LLMs as backends. StructFuse produces a specialized retrieval encoder that excels at the code search task without LLM inference, making it deployable as a lightweight, efficient first-stage retriever in multi-stage pipelines.\\n\\n## Related Work 段落草稿 (~200 words)\\n\\n**Graph-Based Methods for Code Understanding.**\\nRecent work has explored leveraging code graphs to enhance LLM-based software engineering. Inference-time approaches construct and query graphs during each interaction: CodexGraph (Liu et al., NAACL 2025) builds a code graph database that LLM agents query via structured interfaces; RepoGraph (Ouyang et al., ICLR 2025) provides repository-level navigation as a plug-in module for SE agents; LocAgent (ACL 2025) and RANGER (Shah et al., 2025) employ graph-guided multi-hop reasoning and MCTS-based traversal for code localization and retrieval, respectively. GraphCoder (ASE 2024) incorporates intra-procedural control-flow and data-dependence graphs for code completion retrieval, while ARISE (Seddik & Fard, 2026) extends this with statement-level def-use edges for fault localization. CodeGRAG (2024) bridges training and inference by using GNN-encoded graph prompts.\\n\\nThese methods achieve strong results but impose substantial inference-time costs—graph construction, traversal, and often multiple LLM calls per query. Moreover, their data-flow analysis remains intra-procedural. StructFuse takes a fundamentally different approach: we leverage CodeQL's inter-procedural taint tracking to generate structure-grounded hard negatives at **training time**, producing an encoder that captures cross-function data-flow semantics without any graph overhead at inference. This positions StructFuse as complementary to inference-time graph methods—it can serve as an efficient first-stage retriever whose embeddings already encode deep structural relationships that other methods must compute on-the-fly.\\n\\n---\\n\\n## 参考文献（全部已验证 2026-05-14）\\n\\n- CodexGraph: arXiv:2408.03910, Liu et al., NAACL 2025\\n- LocAgent: arXiv:2503.09089, Chen et al., ACL 2025\\n- RANGER: arXiv:2509.25257, Shah et al., 2025 ✅ verified\\n- RepoGraph: arXiv:2410.14684, Ouyang et al., ICLR 2025\\n- GraphCoder: arXiv:2406.07003, Liu et al., ASE 2024\\n- CodeRAG: arXiv:2504.10046, 2025\\n- ARISE: arXiv:2605.03117, Seddik & Fard, 2026 ✅ verified (full title: \\\"ARISE: A Repository-level Graph Representation and Toolset for Agentic Fault Localization and Program Repair\\\")\\n- CodeGRAG: build_m1_training_data.py 实现差异 [2026-05-15]\\n\\n**发现者**：Reviewer\\n**影响**：当前 M2 不受影响（用 pre-mined 数据），plan_015（dynamic negative mining）实施前必须对齐\\n\\n**差异**：\\n\\n### hard_negative_mining.py `DataFlowAwareMiner`（training/hard_negative_mining.py）\\n三步法：\\n1. **Embedding top-K**：调用 `EmbeddingDistanceMiner.mine()` 获取 top-50 cosine similarity 最高的 candidates（从所有 non-positive candidates 中）\\n2. **Dataflow filter**：过滤掉 `_is_reachable(query, fid)` 为 True 的文件——仅检查 `dataflow_graph[src]` 中的直接条目（1-hop 可达性）\\n3. **BM25 re-rank**：如果 `bm25_index` 可用，对剩余 disconnected candidates 按 BM25 分数降序重排\\n4. **Fallback**：如果 disconnected 不足 `num_negatives`，用 random padding 补足\\n\\n### build_m1_training_data.py `build_candidate_pools()`（scripts/build_m1_training_data.py）\\n两步法：\\n1. **Dataflow filter**：从 `non_pos_files` 中排除 `full_connected_set`（= `positive_files ∪ cg_connected`，即 dataflow 直连 + call graph 邻居——比 `DataFlowAwareMiner` 排除范围更广）\\n2. **BM25 sort**：对 disconnected 文件按 BM25 分数降序排序，取 StructFuse amortizes all graph computation to training time—once the encoder is trained with structure-grounded negatives, inference is a single embedding lookup with zero graph overhead. This makes StructFuse suitable for latency-sensitive production retrieval where inference-time graph methods are impractical.\\\\n\\\\n2. **Data-flow depth**: Most inference-time methods use structural graphs (call graphs, class hierarchies, file dependencies). Only GraphCoder and ARISE incorporate data/control flow, but limited to **intra-procedural** scope. StructFuse uniquely leverages **inter-procedural** data-flow via CodeQL's taint tracking, capturing cross-function semantic relationships (e.g., a sanitizer function that transforms data between source and sink across call boundaries). This deeper semantic signal generates harder, more informative negatives.\\\\n\\\\n3. **Complementarity**: StructFuse and inference-time methods are orthogonal and composable. A StructFuse-trained encoder can serve as the embedding backbone for systems like RANGER or CodexGraph, providing better initial retrieval that reduces the graph agent's search space. Conversely, inference-time graph methods can provide reranking on top of StructFuse retrieval for tasks requiring maximum precision.\\\\n\\\\n4. **Scalability model**: Inference-time methods scale linearly with query volume (each query triggers graph operations). StructFuse's cost is front-loaded: CodeQL analysis runs once per repository update, and the resulting negatives improve the encoder permanently. For high-QPS code search services, this one-time investment yields compounding returns.\\\\n\\\\n5. **Task generality vs specialization**: Inference-time graph agents (CodexGraph, RepoGraph) are task-agnostic but require powerful LLMs as backends. StructFuse produces a specialized retrieval encoder that excels at the code search task without LLM inference, making it deployable as a lightweight, efficient first-stage retriever in multi-stage pipelines.\\\\n\\\\n## Related Work 段落草稿 (~200 words)\\\\n\\\\n**Graph-Based Methods for Code Understanding.**\\\\nRecent work has explored leveraging code graphs to enhance LLM-based software engineering. Inference-time approaches construct and query graphs during each interaction: CodexGraph (Liu et al., NAACL 2025) builds a code graph database that LLM agents query via structured interfaces; RepoGraph (Ouyang et al., ICLR 2025) provides repository-level navigation as a plug-in module for SE agents; LocAgent (ACL 2025) and RANGER (Shah et al., 2025) employ graph-guided multi-hop reasoning and MCTS-based traversal for code localization and retrieval, respectively. GraphCoder (ASE 2024) incorporates intra-procedural control-flow and data-dependence graphs for code completion retrieval, while ARISE (Seddik & Fard, 2026) extends this with statement-level def-use edges for fault localization. CodeGRAG (2024) bridges training and inference by using GNN-encoded graph prompts.\\\\n\\\\nThese methods achieve strong results but impose substantial inference-time costs—graph construction, traversal, and often multiple LLM calls per query. Moreover, their data-flow analysis remains intra-procedural. StructFuse takes a fundamentally different approach: we leverage CodeQL's inter-procedural taint tracking to generate structure-grounded hard negatives at **training time**, producing an encoder that captures cross-function data-flow semantics without any graph overhead at inference. This positions StructFuse as complementary to inference-time graph methods—it can serve as an efficient first-stage retriever whose embeddings already encode deep structural relationships that other methods must compute on-the-fly.\\\\n\\\\n---\\\\n\\\\n## 参考文献（全部已验证 2026-05-14）\\\\n\\\\n- CodexGraph: arXiv:2408.03910, Liu et al., NAACL 2025\\\\n- LocAgent: arXiv:2503.09089, Chen et al., ACL arXiv:2504.10046, 2025\\\\n- ARISE: arXiv:2605.03117, Seddik & Fard, 2026 ✅ verified (full title: \\\\\\\"ARISE: A Repository-level Graph Representation and Toolset for Agentic Fault Localization and (50%) val_loss 与 R@1 选中不同 checkpoint。\\\\n\\\\n**分歧 pattern**：\\\\n- Qwen s43: val_loss→step 500, R@1→step 1500, R@1 差 +1.97pp\\\\n- StarCoder s42: val_loss→step 1000, R@1→step 2500, R@1 差 +0.64pp\\\\n- Mean delta: +0.65pp (R@1 criterion 优于 val_loss criterion)\\\\n- StarCoder 步差更大（1500 步 vs 1000 步），与其 pretrained margin 更大的特性一致\\\\n\\\\n**论文写作决策**：主表报 best_by_R@1（target metric aligned），ablation 表展示两种 criterion 对比（D036 已决策 R@1 为 primary）。\\\\n\\\\n**K=7 无 ablation**：所有正式实验固定 K=7。合理性来源：(1) batch_size=8 对齐（1 positive + 7 negatives = 8）(2) DPR 等文献惯例 (3) hard_negative_ratio=0.5 平衡设计。K 敏感性列为 limitation/future work。\\\\n\\\\n---\\\\n\\\\n## BM25 Negative Mining 策略洞察 (2026-05-14, updated)\\\\n\\\\n**两层重叠率数据（注意区分）**：\\\\n- **k=50 全候选重叠**（mining worker 报告）：BM25 vs embedding (50-200, 29 repos) | 0.380 | 0.067 | 0.363 |\\\\n| Large (200+, 15 repos) | 0.297 | 0.007 | 0.405 |\\\\n\\\\n**核心修正**：之前基于 87% 预测\\\\\\\"Phase 2 中 BM25 R@1 很可能接近 embedding\\\\\\\"——这个预测可能不成立。在中大 repo（训练数据主体）上 top-7 Jaccard 仅 30-38%，BM25 和 embedding 选出的训练 negatives 大部分不同。Phase 2 训练结果将真正区分两种策略。\\\\n\\\\n**意外发现**：BM25 vs dataflow 在大 repo 上重叠率（0.405）高于 vs embedding（0.297）。词汇相似性信号与数据流依赖有部分对齐，而纯 embedding 距离选择了不同维度的 hard negatives。\\\\n\\\\n---\\\\n\\\\n## hard_negative_mining.py vs build_m1_training_data.py 实现差异 [2026-05-15]\\\\n\\\\n**发现者**：Reviewer\\\\n**影响**：当前 M2 不受影响（用 pre-mined num_negatives=7，分层分析）：差异远大于全候选\\n\\n**分层 top-7 Jaccard 重叠率**（seed 42, 2920 matched queries / 12290 total）：\\n| 层级 | BM25 vs Embedding | BM25 vs Random | BM25 vs Dataflow |\\n|------|-------------------|----------------|------------------|\\n| Small (<50 files, 39 repos) | 0.668 | 0.411 | 0.667 |\\n| Medium (50-200, 29 repos) | 0.380 | 0.067 | 0.363 |\\n| Large (200+, 15 repos) | 0.297 | 0.007 | 0.405 |\\n\\n**核心修正**：之前基于 87% 预测\\\"Phase 2 中 BM25 R@1 很可能接近 embedding\\\"——这个预测可能不成立。在中大 repo（训练数据主体）上 top-7 Jaccard 仅 30-38%，BM25 和 embedding 选出的训练 negatives 大部分不同。Phase 2 训练结果将真正区分两种策略。\\n\\n**意外发现**：BM25 vs dataflow 在大 repo 上重叠率（0.405）高于 vs embedding（0.297）。词汇相似性信号与数据流依赖有部分对齐，而纯 embedding 距离选择了不同维度的 hard negatives。\\n\\n---\\n\\n## hard_negative_mining.py vs build_m1_training_data.py 实现差异 [2026-05-15]\\n\\n**发现者**：Reviewer\\n**影响**：当前 M2 不受影响（用 pre-mined 数据），plan_015（dynamic negative mining）实施前必须对齐\\n\\n**差异**：\\n\\n### hard_negative_mining.py `DataFlowAwareMiner`（training/hard_negative_mining.py）\\n三步法：\\n1. **Embedding top-K**：调用 `EmbeddingDistanceMiner.mine()` 获取 top-50 cosine similarity 最高的 candidates（从所有 non-positive candidates 中）\\n2. **Dataflow filter**：过滤掉 `_is_reachable(query, fid)` 为 True 的文件——仅检查 `dataflow_graph[src]` 中的直接条目（1-hop 可达性）\\n3. **BM25 re-rank**：如果 `bm25_index` 可用，对剩余 disconnected candidates 按 BM25 分数降序重排\\n4. **Fallback**：如果 disconnected 不足 `num_negatives`，用 random padding 补足\\n\\n### build_m1_training_data.py `build_candidate_pools()`（scripts/build_m1_training_data.py）\\n两步法：\\n1. **Dataflow filter**：从 `non_pos_files` 中排除 `full_connected_set`（= `positive_files ∪ cg_connected`，即 dataflow 直连 + call graph 邻居——比 `DataFlowAwareMiner` 排除范围更广）\\n2. **BM25 sort**：对 disconnected 文件按 BM25 分数降序排序，取 top-K（pool_size=50）\\n3. **无 embedding 步骤**：dataflow pool 完全不使用 embedding 信号\\n\\n额外注意：当 `--model-path` 未提供时，`emb_scores = bm25`（line 317-318），此时 embedding pool 也退化为 BM25 排序，仅在是否排除 `full_connected` 上与 dataflow pool 不同。\\n\\n### 差异总结\\n\\n| 维度 | hard_negative_mining.py | build_m1_training_data.py |\\n|------|------------------------|--------------------------|\\n| Candidate 初筛 | Embedding cosine top-50 | 全部 non-positive（无初筛） |\\n| Dataflow 排除范围 | `dataflow_graph[src]` 直接条目 | `positive_files ∪ cg_connected`（更广） |\\n| 排序信号 | Embedding 为主 → BM25 re-rank | 纯 BM25 排序 |\\n| Embedding 角色 | 主选择器（决定候选池） | 不参与 dataflow pool |\\n| BM25 角色 | 可选的 re-ranker | 唯一排序信号 |\\n\\n### 对齐建议\\nplan_015 实施 dynamic negative mining 时需统一为一种逻辑。建议：\\n1. **排除范围统一**：采用 `build_m1_training_data.py` 的 `full_connected`（dataflow + call graph），因为 call graph 邻居也不应作为 hard negatives\\n2. **选择信号**：dynamic mining 应使用当前 epoch 的 encoder embedding（不是 pretrained），这是 dynamic mining 的核心价值——所以 `DataFlowAwareMiner` 的 embedding→filter→BM25 三步法更合理，但需将排除范围从 1-hop dataflow 扩展到 full_connected\\n3. **具体\\n\\n... [截断: 省略 963 字符，传 max_chars=0 获取完整内容]\", \"total_chars\": 12963, \"truncated\": true}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"
